Run 39033000278 (afab218) reached the compute nodes and every decode DP
rank stopped in the qwen3-sm90-megamoe patch's eligibility guard, which
required speculative_num_draft_tokens == 4 on the decode path. An audit
of the integration found no other dependence on four verify rows per
request: forward_megamoe sizes from hidden_states.shape[0] and the
per-rank token cap (4096, far above 32 x 3), the SM90 routed run and the
symmetric buffer take num_tokens at runtime, the exact-tile patch rounds
each expert's valid_m to a multiple of 8 from the data, the compiled
geometry carries no draft term, the decode and draft-extend CUDA graph
runners derive captured_req_width from speculative_num_draft_tokens, and
the staged-EAGLE, handoff and layout patches work per token. The GPU
numeric gate already covers 1..65-row tails. The guard therefore becomes
speculative_num_draft_tokens in (3, 4); SGLang's own top-k-1 rule keeps
draft = steps + 1. check-firmus-megamoe-source.py now asserts that 3 and
4 pass and 2 and 8 fail against the real guard text. All fifteen SGLang
patches still apply in build order to the pinned fc275674 source, and
the staging, MegaMoE-source and latency-source contract checks pass on
the patched tree.

This changes a container input, so the image is rebuilt; nothing in the
DeepGEMM kernels, their patches or the build geometry changes, and the
image header SHA-256 stays 0e34a083.... Benchmark conditions are those
of afab218: concurrency 192, prefill round_robin, decode total_requests,
decode cap 128 with graph buckets to 32 and 16 extra slots, decode
dist-init on 29000, EAGLE3 steps 2 / draft tokens 3, separate profile
pass. Expected effect and acceptance are unchanged: ITL about -8% from
three verify rows per request instead of four, ITL failures near zero,
throughput back to the prefill bound, score about 7000, outputs
unchanged under greedy verification; on an ITL regression revert to
steps 3 / draft 4 and keep the relaxed guard. Ruff and the full test
suite pass. Audit and change by a delegated agent, reviewed before
submission.
